# MostlyText third-party notices

MostlyText is free software under the MIT licence, continuing the MIT
licence of MacDown, from which it descends. Its own licence and
copyright lines are in `LICENSE`.

MostlyText also includes the third-party components below. Each keeps its
own licence. The full text each licence requires is reproduced after the
summary. Where a licence requires source code to be available, the
location is listed with the component.

This file is generated from `compliance/inventory.json` by
`compliance/tools/compliance.py notices`. Do not edit it by hand.

project.  If you have contributed a patch or made some other contribution to

done!

The list is sorted by surname and formatted to allow easy grepping and
beautification by scripts.  The fields are: name (N), email (E), web-address
(W), PGP key ID and fingerprint (P), description (D), and snail-mail address
(S).

E: cvanvliet@auroraux.org

### NOTICE.txt

```text
d2-browser.js is @terrastruct/d2 0.1.33 (https://github.com/d2lang/d2),
MPL-2.0 licensed (D2-LICENSE.txt), with exactly one modification from
upstream: the trailing `export{lw as D2}` ES-module export statement is
replaced with `window.D2=lw;`.

Why: the upstream file is a genuine ES module. Loading it via
`<script type="module">` and an internal `import('./d2-browser.js')` —
the natural approach for an ES module — fails under WebKit's `file://`
loading model with "Cross-origin script load denied by Cross-Origin
Resource Sharing policy," even though the containing page loads fine via
`loadFileURL(_:allowingReadAccessTo:)` and the top-level module script
itself executes. This is a real, current WebKit restriction on ES module
sub-imports specifically, not on ordinary `<script src>` resource
loading. Turning the file into a plain global-assigning script sidesteps
it entirely, matching how every other bundled JS library in this app
(Mermaid, viz-js) is already loaded.

No other change is made — no minification, no other content edit — so
the WASM payload and D2 version are exactly upstream's own 0.1.33 build.

MPL-2.0 is weak, file-level copyleft: a modified MPL-covered file's
source must stay available if distributed, which it already is — this
is a plain, readable, committed resource in this repository, and this
NOTICE documents the exact one-line change rather than leaving it to be
discovered in a diff. See planning/epic-21-implementation.md §1.1 and
the Slice 0 as-built note for the full investigation this NOTICE was
written from.
```

This file is a partial list of people who have contributed to the LLVM/libc++
project.  If you have contributed a patch or made some other contribution to
LLVM/libc++, please submit a patch to this file to add yourself, and it will be
done!

The list is sorted by surname and formatted to allow easy grepping and
beautification by scripts.  The fields are: name (N), email (E), web-address
(W), PGP key ID and fingerprint (P), description (D), and snail-mail address
(S).

## SwiftUIMath

- Version: 2a89c2b4
- Upstream: https://github.com/Joncallim/swiftui-math
- Revision: `2a89c2b41482988711909572d116374e47a52164`
- Licence: MIT
- Copyright (c) 2026 Guille Gonzalez
- Copyright (c) 2023 Computer Inspirations (SwiftMath)
- Copyright (c) 2013 MathChat (iosMath)
- Local modifications: Narrow child of upstream 0.1.0: preserve authored TeX comments and control-word boundaries, expose existing parser diagnostics with original-body UTF-16 cursors and required inspection limits, and reject truncated optional alignment and mismatched radical degrees safely. All fonts, licences, original tests and unrelated files are unchanged. Validated-body child adds an opaque exact-byte token, raw-body view/metrics overloads and mode/limit-aware lock-owned bounded atom/display caches that preserve automatic memory-pressure eviction. No font, licence or other renderer source changes.
